 400 |     const text=this.get(run.owner,'source',run.source_ids[0]).text.trim().replace(/[，。！!,.\s]/g,'').toLowerCase();
 401 |     // Match the entire consent utterance; mentioning generation is not authorization.
 402 |     const affirmative=/^(好的?|可以|行|是的?|同意|确认|没问题|yes|ok|okay|sure)$/.test(text);
 403 |     const request=/^(好的?|可以|行|是的?|同意|确认|没问题)?(请|麻烦)?(现在|就)?(帮我|给我)?(按照?刚才说的)?(生成|整理|开始生成|开始整理)(一份|一张|一个)?(医生沟通卡|就诊沟通卡|沟通卡|健康报告|报告|卡片)?(吧)?(谢谢)?$/.test(text)
 404 |       || /^(好的?|可以)?(现在)?开始吧$/.test(text)
 405 |       || /^(yes|ok|okay|sure)?(please)?generate(a|the)?(doctorcard|visitcard|healthreport|report|card)?(please)?$/.test(text);
 406 |     check(text.length<=100 && (affirmative||request),'INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT',422);
 407 |     const later=this.all('run',run.owner).filter(r=>r.workflow==='chat'&&r.conversation_id===run.conversation_id);
 408 |     check(later.at(-1)?.id===run.id,'INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT',422);
 409 |     // A retry is a new run, not a new user turn: a subsequent refusal supersedes it.
 410 |     check(this.conversationSources(run.owner,run.conversation_id).at(-1)?.id===run.source_ids[0],'INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT',422);
 411 |     return offer;
 412 |   }
 413 |   enqueueVisitContinuation(run) {
 414 |     if(run.workflow!=='chat'||run.continuation_run_id||run.execution_error||!run.result_id)return;
 415 |     const result=this.get(run.owner,'result',run.result_id);
 416 |     if(result.payload.intent!=='confirm_visit')return;
 417 |     check(result.validity==='current','STALE_DEPENDENCY',409);
 418 |     const offer=this.validateVisitConfirmation(run),uid=run.owner;
 419 |     const confirmation=this.get(uid,'source',run.source_ids[0]).text;
 420 |     const controlOnly=confirmation.replace(/没问题|是的?|医生沟通卡|沟通卡|健康报告|报告|卡片|刚才说的|就按|一份|好的|可以|确认|同意|麻烦|现在|帮我|请|生成|整理|开始|谢谢|好的?|行|吧|就|按|yes|okay|ok|sure|please|generate|[，。！!,.\s]/gi,'')==='';
 421 |     const sources=this.conversationSources(uid,run.conversation_id).filter(source=>!controlOnly||source.id!==run.source_ids[0]),text=this.text(sources.map(s=>s.text).join('\n'));
 422 |     const selected=[...new Set([...run.source_ids.filter(sid=>!controlOnly||sid!==run.source_ids[0]),...sources.map(s=>s.id)])];
 423 |     check(selected.length<this.limits.retrievalCount,'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',413);
 424 |     check(Buffer.byteLength(text)+selected.reduce((n,sid)=>n+Buffer.byteLength(this.get(uid,'source',sid).text),0)<=this.limits.textBytes*2,'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',413);
 425 |     this.checkQueueAdmission(uid);
 426 |     const source=this.insert(uid,'source',{kind:'self_report',text,text_version:1,content_hash:hash(text),recorded_at:this.now(),pages:[],derived_source_ids:sources.map(s=>s.id),input_kind:'self_report'});
 427 |     const episode=this.insert(uid,'episode',{source_id:source.id,label:null,status:'pending',created_at:this.now()});
 428 |     const entry=this.insert(uid,'entry',{source_id:source.id,input_kind:source.input_kind,received_at:this.now(),raw_time_text:null,episode_id:episode.id});
 429 |     const child=this.newRun(uid,'visit',[source.id,...selected],{conversation_id:run.conversation_id,parent_run_id:run.id,document_id:null});
 430 |     const operation=this.insert(uid,'operation',{kind:'accept',status:'committed',source_id:source.id,entry_id:entry.id,document_id:null,run_id:child.id,recorded_at:this.now(),saved:true});
 431 |     this.queueRun(child,operation.id);
 432 |     run.continuation_run_id=child.id;
 433 |     offer.visit_offer_consumed_by=run.id;this.save('run',offer);
 434 |   }
 435 |   conversationMessages(run) {
 436 |     if(!run.conversation_id)return [];
 437 |     const messages=[];
 438 |     const results=this.all('result',run.owner).filter(result=>result.kind==='assistant_reply'&&result.conversation_id===run.conversation_id).sort((a,b)=>b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at));
 439 |     for(const source of this.conversationSources(run.owner,run.conversation_id).filter(source=>run.source_ids.includes(source.id))){
 440 |       messages.push({role:'user',text:source.text,source_id:source.id});
 441 |       const response=results.find(result=>result.source_ids[0]===source.id);
 442 |       if(response?.payload.questions?.length)messages.push({role:'assistant',text:response.payload.questions.join('\n'),intent:response.payload.intent,context_only:true});
 443 |     }
 444 |     check(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(messages))<=this.limits.outputBytes,'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',413);
 445 |     return messages;
 446 |   }
 447 |   editVisit(uid,resultId,body,key,expectedEpoch) {
 448 |     return this.transaction(()=>this.receipt(uid,`edit:${resultId}`,key,body,()=>{
 449 |       this.checkExpectedEpoch(uid,expectedEpoch);
 450 |       const result=this.get(uid,"result",resultId);
 451 |       check(result.kind==='visit_preparation',"INVALID_INPUT");
 452 |       // Patient wording is editable even when its evidence needs a fresh review.
 453 |       // Keep the original payload and its stale status unchanged.
 454 |       check(['current','stale'].includes(result.status)&&['current','stale'].includes(result.validity),"STALE_DEPENDENCY",409);
 455 |       keys(body,['speech','questions','expected_edit_revision']);
 456 |       check(Number.isSafeInteger(body.expected_edit_revision)&&body.expected_edit_revision===(result.user_edit?.revision||0),'REVISION_CONFLICT',409);
 457 |       const speech=this.text(body.speech);
 458 |       check(speech.length<=20000&&Array.isArray(body.questions)&&body.questions.length<=20&&body.questions.every(q=>typeof q==='string'&&q.length<=1000),'INVALID_INPUT');
 459 |       result.user_edit={speech,questions:[...body.questions],revision:body.expected_edit_revision+1,edited_at:this.now()};
 460 |       this.save('result',result);return result;
 461 |     }));
 462 |   }
 463 |   accept(uid, workflow, input, key, document = null, expectedEpoch) {
 464 |     return this.transaction(() =>
 465 |       this.receipt(
 466 |         uid,
 467 |         `accept:${workflow}`,
 468 |         key,
 469 |         { input, document: document ? { hash: hash(document.bytes), pages: document.pages } : null },
 470 |         () => {
 471 |           this.checkExpectedEpoch(uid, expectedEpoch);
 472 |           this.requireConsent(uid);
 473 |           check(["entry", "document", "visit", "chat"].includes(workflow));
 474 |           let selected=input.selected_source_ids||[],conversationId=null,concern=input.current_concern,derivedSourceIds=[];
 475 |           check(Array.isArray(selected));
 476 |           if(workflow==='visit'&&input.previous_result_id){
 477 |             const previous=this.get(uid,'result',input.previous_result_id);
 478 |             check(previous.kind==='visit_preparation','INVALID_INPUT');
 479 |             const previousRun=this.get(uid,'run',previous.run_id);
 480 |             check(!previousRun.deleted&&previousRun.source_ids.length,'SOURCE_DELETED',410);
 481 |             concern=this.get(uid,'source',previousRun.source_ids[0]).text;derivedSourceIds=[previousRun.source_ids[0]];
 482 |             selected=previousRun.source_ids.slice(1);conversationId=previousRun.conversation_id||null;
 483 |           }
 484 |           const priorChat=workflow==='chat'?this.all('run',uid).filter(r=>r.workflow==='chat'&&selected.includes(r.source_ids[0])).sort((a,b)=>b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at))[0]:null;
 485 |           conversationId=workflow==='visit'?(input.conversation_id||conversationId):workflow==='chat'?(priorChat?.conversation_id||id('chat')):null;
 486 |           if(conversationId&&(workflow==='visit'||priorChat)){
 487 |             const conversation=this.conversationSources(uid,conversationId);
 488 |             if(workflow==='visit'){concern=conversation.map(source=>source.text).join('\n');derivedSourceIds=[...derivedSourceIds,...conversation.map(source=>source.id)];}
 489 |             selected=[...new Set([...selected,...conversation.map(source=>source.id)])];
 490 |           }
 491 |           const text = this.text(workflow === "visit" ? concern : workflow === "chat" ? input.question : input.text);
 492 |           check(selected.length<(conversationId?this.limits.retrievalCount:this.limits.retrievalCount+1),'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',413);
 493 |           check(Buffer.byteLength(text)+selected.reduce((n,sid)=>n+Buffer.byteLength(this.get(uid,'source',sid).text),0)<=this.limits.textBytes*2,'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',413);
 494 |           this.checkQueueAdmission(uid);
 495 |           for (const s of selected) this.get(uid, "source", s);
 496 |           if (input.episode_id) this.get(uid, "episode", input.episode_id);
 497 |           const source = this.insert(uid, "source", {
 498 |             kind: workflow === "document" ? "medical_document" : "self_report",
 499 |             text,
 500 |             text_version: 1,
 501 |             content_hash: hash(text),
 502 |             recorded_at: this.now(),
 503 |             pages: document?.pages || [],
 504 |             ...(derivedSourceIds.length?{derived_source_ids:[...new Set(derivedSourceIds)]}:{}),
 505 |             input_kind: workflow === "chat" ? "assistant_question" : document ? "pdf" : workflow === "document" ? "pasted_document" : "self_report",
 506 |           });
 507 |           const episode = input.episode_id
 508 |             ? this.get(uid, "episode", input.episode_id)
 509 |             : this.insert(uid, "episode", {
 510 |                 source_id: source.id,
 511 |                 label: null,
 512 |                 status: "pending",
 513 |                 created_at: this.now(),
 514 |               });
 515 |           const entry = this.insert(uid, "entry", {
 516 |             source_id: source.id,
 517 |             input_kind: source.input_kind,
 518 |             received_at: this.now(),
 519 |             raw_time_text: null,
 520 |             episode_id: episode.id,
 521 |           });
 522 |           let doc = null;
 523 |           if (workflow === "document") {
 524 |             doc = this.insert(uid, "document", {
 525 |               source_id: source.id,
 526 |               name: typeof input.name === "string" ? input.name.slice(0, 200) : null,
 527 |               media_type: document ? "application/pdf" : "text/plain",
 528 |               byte_size: document?.bytes.length || Buffer.byteLength(text),
 529 |               content_hash: document ? hash(document.bytes) : source.content_hash,
 530 |               extraction_status: "extracted",
 531 |               pages: source.pages,
 532 |             });
 533 |             if (document)
 534 |               this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO originals VALUES(?,?,?)").run(source.id, uid, document.bytes);
 535 |           }
 536 |           const visitOffer=workflow==='chat'&&conversationId?this.pendingVisitOffer(uid,conversationId):null;
 537 |           const run = this.newRun(uid, workflow, [source.id, ...selected], { ...(visitOffer?{visit_offer_run_id:visitOffer}:{}), document_id: doc?.id || null, ...(conversationId ? {conversation_id:conversationId} : {}) });
 538 |           const operation = this.insert(uid, "operation", {
 539 |             kind: "accept",
 540 |             status: "committed",
 541 |             source_id: source.id,
 542 |             entry_id: entry.id,
 543 |             document_id: doc?.id || null,
 544 |             run_id: run.id,
 545 |             recorded_at: this.now(),
 546 |             saved: true,
 547 |           });
 548 |           this.queueRun(run, operation.id);
 549 |           return {
 550 |             entry_id: entry.id,
 551 |             source_id: source.id,
 552 |             ...(doc ? { document_id: doc.id } : {}),
 553 |             ...(run.conversation_id ? {conversation_id:run.conversation_id} : {}),
 554 |             run_id: run.id,
 555 |             operation_id: operation.id,
 556 |             saved: true,
 557 |           };
 558 |         },
 559 |       ),
 560 |     );
 561 |   }
 562 |   profile(uid) {
 563 |     const u = this.user(uid),
 564 |       revs = this.all("fact_revision", uid),
 565 |       latest = new Map();
 566 |     for (const f of revs)
 567 |       if (!latest.has(f.fact_id) || latest.get(f.fact_id).revision < f.revision) latest.set(f.fact_id, f);
 568 |     return {
 569 |       profile_revision: u.revision,
 570 |       facts: [...latest.values()].filter((x) => x.review_status === "confirmed"),
 571 |       pending_changes: this.all("fact_change", uid).filter((x) => x.status === "pending"),
 572 |     };
 573 |   }
 574 |   timeline(uid) {
 575 |     this.user(uid);
 576 |     return {
 577 |       entries: this.all("entry", uid).map((e) => ({ ...e, source: this.get(uid, "source", e.source_id) })),
 578 |     };
 579 |   }
 580 |   claimRun(runId) {
 581 |     return this.transaction(() => {
 582 |       const r = this.internalRun(runId);
 583 |       if (r.status !== "queued") return null;
 584 |       if (r.deadline <= this.clock()) {
 585 |         r.status = "failed";
 586 |         r.phase = "dispatch";
 587 |         r.error = runError("DEADLINE_EXCEEDED", "dispatch");
 588 |         this.save("run", r);
 589 |         this.finishDispatch(r);
 590 |         return null;
 591 |       }
 592 |       try {
 593 |         this.checkScope(r);
 594 |       } catch (e) {
 595 |         r.status = "cancelled";
 596 |         r.error = runError(e.code, "scope");
 597 |         this.save("run", r);
 598 |         this.finishDispatch(r);
 599 |         return null;
 600 |       }
 601 |       r.status = "running";
 602 |       r.phase = "agent";
 603 |       this.save("run", r);
 604 |       const d = this.all("dispatch", r.owner).find((d) => d.run_id === r.id);
 605 |       d.state = "running";
 606 |       d.attempt++;
 607 |       d.version++;
 608 |       this.save("dispatch", d);
 609 |       return r;
 610 |     });
 611 |   }
 612 |   finishDispatch(run) {
 613 |     const d = this.all("dispatch", run.owner).find((x) => x.run_id === run.id);
 614 |     if (d) {
 615 |       d.state = run.status;
 616 |       d.version++;
 617 |       this.save("dispatch", d);
 618 |     }
 619 |   }
 620 |   checkScope(run, scope) {
 621 |     const current = this.internalRun(run.id),
 622 |       u = this.user(current.owner),
 623 |       c = this.consent(current.owner);
 624 |     check(
 625 |       active.includes(current.status),
 626 |       current.status === "cancelled" ? "RUN_CANCELLED" : "SCOPE_REVOKED",
 627 |       409,
 628 |     );
 629 |     check(
 630 |       c.granted &&
 631 |         c.version === current.consent_version &&
 632 |         u.epoch === current.epoch &&
 633 |         this.clock() < current.expires_at &&
 634 |         (!scope || scope.scope_epoch === current.scope_epoch),
 635 |       "SCOPE_REVOKED",
 636 |       403,
 637 |     );
 638 |     for (const sid of current.source_ids) this.get(current.owner, "source", sid);
 639 |     return current;
 640 |   }
 641 |   reserveRuntime(runId) {
 642 |     return this.transaction(() => {
 643 |       const r = this.checkScope(this.internalRun(runId));
 644 |       check(r.status === "running", "SCOPE_REVOKED", 403);
 645 |       check(!this.all("runtime_binding", r.owner).some((b) => b.run_id === runId), "SCOPE_REVOKED", 403);
 646 |       const sessionKey = "healththread:" + randomUUID();
 647 |       this.insert(r.owner, "runtime_binding", {
 648 |         run_id: runId,
 649 |         session_key: sessionKey,
 650 |         session_id: null,
 651 |         scope_epoch: r.scope_epoch,
 652 |         profile_revision: r.base_profile_revision,
 653 |         consent_version: r.consent_version,
 654 |         epoch: r.epoch,
 655 |         source_ids: r.source_ids,
 656 |         expires_at: Math.min(r.deadline, r.expires_at),
 657 |         revoked: false,
 658 |         repairs: [],
 659 |       });
 660 |       return sessionKey;
 661 |     });
 662 |   }
 663 |   runtimeBinding(runId, key) {
 664 |     const r = this.internalRun(runId),
 665 |       b = this.all("runtime_binding", r.owner).find((b) => b.run_id === runId && b.session_key === key);
 666 |     check(b && !b.revoked, "SCOPE_REVOKED", 403);
 667 |     return b;
 668 |   }
 669 |   checkRuntime(runId, key, { unbound = false } = {}) {
 670 |     const r = this.internalRun(runId),
 671 |       b = this.runtimeBinding(runId, key),
 672 |       u = this.user(r.owner),
 673 |       c = this.consent(r.owner);
 674 |     check(
 675 |       (unbound || b.session_id === key) &&
 676 |         ["running", "needs_review", "completed"].includes(r.status) &&
 677 |         !r.deleted &&
 678 |         r.scope_epoch === b.scope_epoch &&
 679 |         u.epoch === b.epoch &&
 680 |         u.revision === b.profile_revision &&
 681 |         c.granted &&
 682 |         c.version === b.consent_version &&
 683 |         this.clock() < b.expires_at,
 684 |       "SCOPE_REVOKED",
 685 |       403,
 686 |     );
 687 |     for (const sid of b.source_ids) this.get(r.owner, "source", sid);
 688 |     return b;
 689 |   }
 690 |   bindRuntimeSession(runId, key, sessionId) {
 691 |     return this.transaction(() => {
 692 |       const b = this.checkRuntime(runId, key, { unbound: true });
 693 |       check(sessionId === key && !b.session_id, "SCOPE_REVOKED", 403);
 694 |       b.session_id = sessionId;
 695 |       this.save("runtime_binding", b);
 696 |       return sessionId;
 697 |     });
 698 |   }
 699 |   revokeRuntime(runId, key) {
 700 |     return this.transaction(() => {
 701 |       const r = this.internalRun(runId),
 702 |         b = this.all("runtime_binding", r.owner).find((b) => b.run_id === runId && b.session_key === key);
 703 |       if (b) {
 704 |         b.revoked = true;
 705 |         this.save("runtime_binding", b);
 706 |       }
 707 |     });
 708 |   }
 709 |   recordRuntimeRepair(runId, key, rejection) {
 710 |     const exceeded = this.transaction(() => {
 711 |       const b = this.checkRuntime(runId, key);
 712 |       if (b.repairs.includes(rejection)) return false;
 713 |       check(typeof rejection === "string" && rejection.length < 100, "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
 714 |       if (b.repairs.length >= 2) {
 715 |         b.revoked = true;
 716 |         this.save("runtime_binding", b);
 717 |         return true;
 718 |       }
 719 |       b.repairs.push(rejection);
 720 |       this.save("runtime_binding", b);
 721 |       const r = this.internalRun(runId);
 722 |       r.repair_attempts = b.repairs.length;
 723 |       this.save("run", r);
 724 |       return false;
 725 |     });
 726 |     if (exceeded) fail(422, "OUTPUT_REPAIR_EXHAUSTED");
 727 |   }
 728 |   runtimeStageOutcome(runId, key, name, args, receiptId) {
 729 |     const binding = this.checkRuntime(runId, key),
 730 |       run = this.internalRun(runId);
 731 |     check(
 732 |       ["healththread_stage_candidates", "healththread_stage_result"].includes(name),
 733 |       "AGH_RECEIPT_UNCORRELATED",
 734 |       422,
 735 |     );
 736 |     const operation = (name === "healththread_stage_candidates" ? "stage:" : "result:") + runId;
 737 |     const saved = this.db
 738 |       .prepare("SELECT request_hash,outcome FROM receipts WHERE owner=? AND operation=? AND key=?")
 739 |       .get(run.owner, operation, args.operation_key);
 740 |     const receipt = this.get(run.owner, "tool_receipt", receiptId);
 741 |     check(
 742 |       saved &&
 743 |         saved.request_hash === hash(canonical(args)) &&
 744 |         receipt.run_id === runId &&
 745 |         receipt.tool === name &&
 746 |         receipt.agh_session_id === binding.session_id &&
 747 |         receipt.args_hash === saved.request_hash,
 748 |       "AGH_RECEIPT_UNCORRELATED",
 749 |       422,
 750 |     );
 751 |     return JSON.parse(saved.outcome);
 752 |   }
 753 |   recordRuntimeDiagnostic(runId, key, diagnostic) {
 754 |     return this.transaction(() => {
 755 |       const r = this.internalRun(runId),
 756 |         b = this.all("runtime_binding", r.owner).find((b) => b.run_id === runId && b.session_key === key);
 757 |       if (b) {
 758 |         b.diagnostic = structuredClone(diagnostic);
 759 |         this.save("runtime_binding", b);
 760 |       }
 761 |     });
 762 |   }
 763 |   recordRuntimeEvidence(runId, key, evidence) {
 764 |     return this.transaction(() => {
 765 |       const b = this.checkRuntime(runId, key);
 766 |       b.evidence = structuredClone(evidence);
 767 |       this.save("runtime_binding", b);
 768 |     });
 769 |   }
 770 |   toolsFor(runId, invocation) {
 771 |     const scope = this.internalRun(runId);
 772 |     const call = (name, args, fn, write = false) => {
 773 |       args = structuredClone(args);
 774 |       const execute = () => {
 775 |         const current = this.internalRun(scope.id);
 776 |         if (invocation) {
 777 |           this.checkRuntime(scope.id, invocation.session_key);
 778 |           check(
 779 |             typeof invocation.tool_use_id === "string" && invocation.tool_use_id.length > 0,
 780 |             "SCOPE_REVOKED",
 781 |             403,
 782 |           );
 783 |         }
 784 |         if (write && args?.operation_key) {
 785 |           const operation = `${name === "healththread_stage_candidates" ? "stage" : "result"}:${scope.id}`;
 786 |           const previous = this.db
 787 |             .prepare("SELECT * FROM receipts WHERE owner=? AND operation=? AND key=?")
 788 |             .get(scope.owner, operation, args.operation_key);
 789 |           if (previous) {
 790 |             const u = this.user(scope.owner),
 791 |               c = this.consent(scope.owner);
 792 |             check(
 793 |               c.granted &&
 794 |                 c.version === current.consent_version &&
 795 |                 u.epoch === current.epoch &&
 796 |                 scope.scope_epoch === current.scope_epoch &&
 797 |                 !current.deleted &&
 798 |                 this.clock() < current.expires_at &&
 799 |                 ["needs_review", "completed", "running"].includes(current.status),
 800 |               "SCOPE_REVOKED",
 801 |               403,
 802 |             );
 803 |             check(previous.request_hash === hash(canonical(args)), "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", 409);
 804 |             const receipt = this.all("tool_receipt", scope.owner).find(
 805 |               (x) => x.run_id === scope.id && x.tool === name,
 806 |             );
 807 |             return { ...JSON.parse(previous.outcome), receipt };
 808 |           }
 809 |         }
 810 |         const run = this.checkScope(scope, scope);
 811 |         check(run.status === "running", "SCOPE_REVOKED", 409);
 812 |         check(
 813 |           args &&
 814 |             typeof args === "object" &&
 815 |             !Object.keys(args).some((k) => ["owner", "user_id", "token", "path"].includes(k)),
 816 |           "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT",
 817 |           422,
 818 |         );
 819 |         const out = fn(run, args);
 820 |         const latest = this.internalRun(run.id);
 821 |         if (out.source) latest.read_sources = [...new Set([...(latest.read_sources || []), out.source.id])];
 822 |         if (out.facts)
 823 |           latest.read_facts = [
 824 |             ...(latest.read_facts || []),
 825 |             ...out.facts.map((f) => ({ fact_id: f.fact_id, revision: f.revision })),
 826 |           ].filter(
 827 |             (f, i, a) => a.findIndex((x) => x.fact_id === f.fact_id && x.revision === f.revision) === i,
 828 |           );
 829 |         if (out.sources)
 830 |           latest.read_sources = [
 831 |             ...new Set([...(latest.read_sources || []), ...out.sources.map((s) => s.id)]),
 832 |           ];
 833 |         if (out.instructions)
 834 |           latest.read_facts = out.instructions.map((f) => ({ fact_id: f.fact_id, revision: f.revision }));
 835 |         const receipt = this.insert(run.owner, "tool_receipt", {
 836 |           tool: name,
 837 |           run_id: run.id,
 838 |           operation_id: out.operation_id || id("op"),
 839 |           snapshot_revision: this.user(run.owner).revision,
 840 |           recorded_at: this.now(),
 841 |           validation: "passed",
 842 |           ...(invocation
 843 |             ? {
 844 |                 agh_session_id: invocation.session_key,
 845 |                 agh_tool_use_id: invocation.tool_use_id,
 846 |                 args_hash: hash(canonical(args)),
 847 |               }
 848 |             : {}),
 849 |         });
 850 |         latest.tool_receipts.push(receipt.id);
 851 |         this.save("run", latest);
 852 |         return { ...out, receipt };
 853 |       };
 854 |       return write ? this.transaction(execute) : this.transaction(execute);
 855 |     };
 856 |     const read = (run, sid) => {
 857 |       check(run.source_ids.includes(sid), "NOT_FOUND", 404);
 858 |       return this.get(run.owner, "source", sid);
 859 |     };
 860 |     return Object.freeze({
 861 |       healththread_read_source: (args) =>
 862 |         call("healththread_read_source", args, (r, a) => {
 863 |           keys(a, ["source_id"]);
 864 |           const source = read(r, a.source_id);
 865 |           return {
 866 |             source,
 867 |             sentence_spans: sentenceSpans(source),
 868 |             sentence_annotations: sentenceAnnotations(source),
 869 |           };
 870 |         }),
 871 |       healththread_retrieve_context: (args) =>
 872 |         call("healththread_retrieve_context", args, (r, a) => {
 873 |           keys(a, ["query", "episode_id", "source_ids"], []);
 874 |           check(!a.query || (typeof a.query === "string" && a.query.length <= 1000));
 875 |           const ids = a.source_ids || (r.conversation_id ? r.source_ids : []);
 876 |           check(Array.isArray(ids) && ids.length <= this.limits.retrievalCount);
 877 |           if (a.episode_id) this.get(r.owner, "episode", a.episode_id);
 878 |           const p = this.profile(r.owner),
 879 |             sources = ids.map((s) => read(r, s));
 880 |           const query = (a.query || "").normalize("NFKC").toLowerCase().trim();
 881 |           const facts = p.facts.filter(
 882 |             (f) =>
 883 |               (!query || f.value.normalize("NFKC").toLowerCase().includes(query)) &&
 884 |               (!a.source_ids?.length || f.evidence.some((e) => ids.includes(e.source_id))) &&
 885 |               (!a.episode_id || f.episode_id === a.episode_id),
 886 |           );
 887 |           return {
 888 |             facts: facts.slice(0, this.limits.retrievalCount),
 889 |             sources,
 890 |             ...(r.conversation_id?{conversation_messages:this.conversationMessages(r),pending_visit_offer:!!r.visit_offer_run_id}:{}),
 891 |             source_spans:sources.map(source=>({source_id:source.id,sentence_spans:sentenceSpans(source),sentence_annotations:sentenceAnnotations(source)})),
 892 |             conflicts: p.pending_changes.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, conflicts: c.conflicts })),
 893 |             snapshot_revision: p.profile_revision,
 894 |           };
 895 |         }),
 896 |       healththread_stage_candidates: (args) =>
 897 |         call("healththread_stage_candidates", args, (r, a) => this.stageCandidates(r, a, read), true),
 898 |       healththread_get_confirmed_instructions: (args) =>
 899 |         call("healththread_get_confirmed_instructions", args, (r, a) => {
 900 |           keys(a, ["document_id"]);
 901 |           check(r.workflow === "care_plan" && a.document_id === r.document_id, "NOT_FOUND", 404);
 902 |           const doc = this.get(r.owner, "document", a.document_id);
 903 |           read(r, doc.source_id);
 904 |           return {
 905 |             instructions: this.profile(r.owner).facts.filter(
 906 |               (f) => f.field === "instruction" && f.evidence.some((e) => e.source_id === doc.source_id),
 907 |             ),
 908 |           };
 909 |         }),
 910 |       healththread_stage_result: (args) =>
 911 |         call("healththread_stage_result", args, (r, a) => this.stageResult(r, a, read), true),
 912 |     });
 913 |   }
 914 |   stageCandidates(run, args, read) {
 915 |     keys(args, ["candidates", "base_profile_revision", "operation_key", "record_dialogue"],["candidates", "base_profile_revision", "operation_key"]);
 916 |     check(["entry", "document"].includes(run.workflow), "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
 917 |     return this.receipt(run.owner, `stage:${run.id}`, args.operation_key, args, () => {
 918 |       check(
 919 |         args.base_profile_revision === run.base_profile_revision &&
 920 |           args.base_profile_revision === this.user(run.owner).revision,
 921 |         "REVISION_CONFLICT",
 922 |         409,
 923 |       );
 924 |       check(Array.isArray(args.candidates) && args.candidates.length <= 100, "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
 925 |       check(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(args)) <= this.limits.outputBytes, "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
 926 |       const readTools = this.all("tool_receipt", run.owner)
 927 |         .filter((x) => x.run_id === run.id)
 928 |         .map((x) => x.tool);
 929 |       check(
 930 |         readTools.includes("healththread_read_source") && readTools.includes("healththread_retrieve_context"),
 931 |         "TOOL_SEQUENCE_REQUIRED",
 932 |         422,
 933 |       );
 934 |       const proposals = args.candidates.map((c, i) => {
 935 |         for (const field of ["fact_id", "episode_id", "expected_version"])
 936 |           if (Object.hasOwn(c, field))
 937 |             fail(422, "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", "新增候选不得自行指定事实标识、版本或事件。", {
 938 |               path: `candidates[${i}].${field}`,
 939 |               rule: "Omit this field for new proposals; server assigns identities.",
 940 |             });
 941 |         check(c.proposal_kind === "new", "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
 942 |         return {
 943 |           ...validateCandidate(c, (s) => {
 944 |             check(run.read_sources?.includes(s), "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
 945 |             return read(run, s);
 946 |           }),
 947 |           id: id("candidate"),
 948 |         };
 949 |       });
 950 |       const spans = new Set();
 951 |       for (const proposal of proposals)
 952 |         for (const evidence of proposal.evidence) {
 953 |           const span = canonical({
 954 |             source_id: evidence.source_id,
 955 |             source_text_version: evidence.source_text_version,
 956 |             locator: evidence.locator,
 957 |             value: proposal.value,
 958 |           });
 959 |           check(!spans.has(span), "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
 960 |           spans.add(span);
 961 |         }
 962 |       const change = this.insert(run.owner, "fact_change", {
 963 |         run_id: run.id,
 964 |         source_ids: run.source_ids,
 965 |         base_profile_revision: args.base_profile_revision,
 966 |         epoch: run.epoch,
 967 |         consent_version: run.consent_version,
 968 |         status: "pending",
 969 |         kind: "new",
 970 |         proposals,
 971 |         conflicts: [],
 972 |         created_at: this.now(),
 973 |         document_id: run.document_id,
 974 |       });
 975 |       const summaryId=run.record_thread_id?stageRecordSummary(this,run,change,args.record_dialogue):null;
 976 |       run.fact_change_id = change.id;
 977 |       run.status = "needs_review";
 978 |       run.phase = "review";
 979 |       this.save("run", run);
 980 |       this.finishDispatch(run);
 981 |       return { fact_change_id: change.id, status: "needs_review",...(summaryId?{record_summary_id:summaryId}:{}) };
 982 |     });
 983 |   }
 984 |   confirm(uid, changeId, body, key, expectedEpoch) {
 985 |     return this.transaction(() =>
 986 |       this.receipt(uid, `confirm:${changeId}`, key, body, () => {
 987 |         this.checkExpectedEpoch(uid, expectedEpoch);
 988 |         const change = this.get(uid, "fact_change", changeId),
 989 |           u = this.user(uid),
 990 |           c = this.requireConsent(uid);
 991 |         keys(body,['accepted_change_ids','expected_profile_revision','record_summary_id','confirmation_message'],['accepted_change_ids','expected_profile_revision']);
 992 |         checkRecordConfirmation(this,uid,change,body);
 993 |         check(change.status === "pending", "REVISION_CONFLICT", 409);
 994 |         check(change.epoch === u.epoch && change.consent_version === c.version, "SCOPE_REVOKED", 403);
 995 |         check(
 996 |           body.expected_profile_revision === u.revision && change.base_profile_revision <= u.revision,
 997 |           "REVISION_CONFLICT",
 998 |           409,
 999 |         );
1000 |         check(
1001 |           Array.isArray(body.accepted_change_ids) &&
1002 |             new Set(body.accepted_change_ids).size === body.accepted_change_ids.length &&
1003 |             body.accepted_change_ids.every((i) => change.proposals.some((p) => p.id === i)),
1004 |         );
1005 |         if (change.run_id) {
1006 |           const run = this.get(uid, "run", change.run_id);
1007 |           check(run.status === "needs_review", "SCOPE_REVOKED", 409);
1008 |         }
1009 |         const selected = change.proposals.filter((p) => body.accepted_change_ids.includes(p.id));
1010 |         for (const sid of change.source_ids) this.get(uid, "source", sid);
1011 |         for (const p of selected) {
1012 |           const clean = { ...p };
1013 |           delete clean.id;
1014 |           validateCandidate(clean, (sid) => this.get(uid, "source", sid));
1015 |           let previous = null;
1016 |           if (p.fact_id) {
1017 |             previous = this.profile(uid).facts.find((f) => f.fact_id === p.fact_id);
1018 |             check(previous && previous.revision === p.expected_version, "REVISION_CONFLICT", 409);
1019 |           }
1020 |           const factId = p.fact_id || id("fact");
1021 |           this.insert(uid, "fact_revision", {
1022 |             ...clean,
1023 |             episode_id:
1024 |               previous?.episode_id ||
1025 |               this.all("entry", uid).find((e) => e.source_id === clean.evidence[0].source_id)?.episode_id ||
1026 |               null,
1027 |             fact_id: factId,
1028 |             revision: (previous?.revision || 0) + 1,
1029 |             supersedes_revision: previous?.revision || null,
1030 |             review_status: p.proposal_kind === "retraction" ? "retracted" : "confirmed",
1031 |             confirmed_at: this.now(),
1032 |             change_id: change.id,
1033 |           });
1034 |         }
1035 |         if (selected.length) this.db.prepare("UPDATE users SET revision=revision+1 WHERE id=?").run(uid);
1036 |         const revision = this.user(uid).revision;
1037 |         change.status = selected.length ? "confirmed" : "rejected";
1038 |         change.accepted_change_ids = body.accepted_change_ids;
1039 |         change.confirmed_at = this.now();
1040 |         this.save("fact_change", change);
1041 |         if (selected.length) this.invalidateProfileDependents(uid);
1042 |         if (change.run_id) {
1043 |           const r = this.get(uid, "run", change.run_id);
1044 |           r.status = "completed";
1045 |           r.phase = "confirmed";
1046 |           this.save("run", r);
1047 |           this.finishDispatch(r);
1048 |         }
1049 |         const audit = this.insert(uid, "audit", {
1050 |           kind: "confirm",
1051 |           change_id: change.id,
1052 |           profile_revision: revision,
1053 |           accepted_change_ids: body.accepted_change_ids,
1054 |           recorded_at: this.now(),
1055 |         });
1056 |         const operation = this.insert(uid, "operation", {
1057 |           kind: "confirm",
1058 |           status: "committed",
1059 |           fact_change_id: change.id,
1060 |           profile_revision: revision,
1061 |           audit_id: audit.id,
1062 |           recorded_at: this.now(),
1063 |         });
1064 |         let continuation = null;
1065 |         if (change.document_id && selected.length) {
1066 |           const doc = this.get(uid, "document", change.document_id);
1067 |           const r = this.newRun(uid, "care_plan", [doc.source_id], {
1068 |             document_id: doc.id,
1069 |             parent_run_id: change.run_id,
1070 |           });
1071 |           this.queueRun(r, operation.id);
1072 |           continuation = r.id;
1073 |         }
1074 |         const outcome = {
1075 |           profile_revision: revision,
1076 |           commit_receipt: operation.id,
1077 |           operation_id: operation.id,
1078 |           ...(continuation ? { continuation_run_id: continuation } : {}),
1079 |         };
1080 |         finishRecordConfirmation(this,uid,change,body,outcome);return outcome;
1081 |       }),
1082 |     );
1083 |   }
1084 |   reject(uid, changeId, body, key, expectedEpoch) {
1085 |     return this.transaction(() =>
1086 |       this.receipt(uid, `reject:${changeId}`, key, body, () => {
1087 |         this.checkExpectedEpoch(uid, expectedEpoch);
1088 |         const change = this.get(uid, "fact_change", changeId),
1089 |           user = this.user(uid),
1090 |           consent = this.requireConsent(uid);
1091 |         check(change.status === "pending", "REVISION_CONFLICT", 409);
1092 |         check(
1093 |           change.epoch === user.epoch && change.consent_version === consent.version,
1094 |           "SCOPE_REVOKED",
1095 |           403,
1096 |         );
1097 |         // Rejection creates no fact: compare the user's current snapshot, not the proposal's old base.
1098 |         check(body.expected_profile_revision === user.revision, "REVISION_CONFLICT", 409);
1099 |         if (change.run_id)
1100 |           check(this.get(uid, "run", change.run_id).status === "needs_review", "SCOPE_REVOKED", 409);
1101 |         change.status = "rejected";
1102 |         change.accepted_change_ids = [];
1103 |         change.confirmed_at = this.now();
1104 |         this.save("fact_change", change);
1105 |         if (change.run_id) {
1106 |           const run = this.get(uid, "run", change.run_id);
1107 |           run.status = "completed";
1108 |           run.phase = "rejected";
1109 |           run.scope_epoch++;
1110 |           this.save("run", run);
1111 |           this.finishDispatch(run);
1112 |         }
1113 |         const audit = this.insert(uid, "audit", {
1114 |           kind: "reject",
1115 |           change_id: change.id,
1116 |           profile_revision: user.revision,
1117 |           recorded_at: this.now(),
1118 |         });
1119 |         const operation = this.insert(uid, "operation", {
1120 |           kind: "reject",
1121 |           status: "committed",
1122 |           fact_change_id: change.id,
1123 |           profile_revision: user.revision,
1124 |           audit_id: audit.id,
1125 |           recorded_at: this.now(),
1126 |         });
1127 |         const outcome = { profile_revision: user.revision, commit_receipt: operation.id, operation_id: operation.id };
1128 |         finishRecordConfirmation(this,uid,change,{accepted_change_ids:[]},outcome);
1129 |         return outcome;
1130 |       }),
1131 |     );
1132 |   }
1133 |   reanalyse(uid, runId, body, key, expectedEpoch) {
1134 |     return this.transaction(() =>
1135 |       this.receipt(uid, `reanalyse:${runId}`, key, body, () => {
1136 |         this.checkExpectedEpoch(uid, expectedEpoch);
1137 |         const old = this.get(uid, "run", runId),
1138 |           user = this.user(uid),
1139 |           consent = this.requireConsent(uid);
1140 |         check(body.expected_profile_revision === user.revision, "REVISION_CONFLICT", 409);
1141 |         check(
1142 |           old.status === "needs_review" && !old.deleted && old.fact_change_id,
1143 |           "RUN_NOT_REANALYSABLE",
1144 |           409,
1145 |         );
1146 |         check(old.epoch === user.epoch && old.consent_version === consent.version, "SCOPE_REVOKED", 403);
1147 |         const change = this.get(uid, "fact_change", old.fact_change_id);
1148 |         check(change.status === "pending", "REVISION_CONFLICT", 409);
1149 |         for (const sid of old.source_ids) this.get(uid, "source", sid);
1150 |         this.checkQueueAdmission(uid);
1151 |         // Expire the old proposal and scope atomically; never rebase its values into current facts.
1152 |         old.status = "cancelled";
1153 |         old.phase = "reanalysed";
1154 |         old.scope_epoch++;
1155 |         this.save("run", old);
1156 |         this.finishDispatch(old);
1157 |         const recordThread=checkRecordRetry(this,uid,old);
1158 |         const run = this.newRun(uid, old.workflow, old.source_ids, {
1159 |           ...(old.record_thread_id?{record_thread_id:old.record_thread_id}:{}),
1160 |           document_id: old.document_id,
1161 |           parent_run_id: old.id,
1162 |         });
1163 |         updateRecordRetry(this,recordThread,run);
1164 |         change.status = "expired";
1165 |         change.terminal_reason = "reanalysed";
1166 |         change.expired_at = this.now();
1167 |         change.superseded_by_run_id = run.id;
1168 |         this.save("fact_change", change);
1169 |         const operation = this.insert(uid, "operation", {
1170 |           kind: "reanalyse",
1171 |           status: "committed",
1172 |           run_id: run.id,
1173 |           previous_run_id: old.id,
1174 |           source_id: old.source_ids[0],
1175 |           expired_fact_change_id: change.id,
1176 |           saved: true,
1177 |           recorded_at: this.now(),
1178 |         });
1179 |         this.queueRun(run, operation.id);
1180 |         return {
1181 |           run_id: run.id,
1182 |           source_id: old.source_ids[0],
1183 |           operation_id: operation.id,
1184 |           expired_fact_change_id: change.id,
1185 |           saved: true,
1186 |         };
1187 |       }),
1188 |     );
1189 |   }
1190 |   invalidate(uid, predicate, status) {
1191 |     for (const type of ["result", "care_task"])
1192 |       for (const item of this.all(type, uid))
1193 |         if (predicate(item)) {
1194 |           item.status = type === "result" ? status : item.status;
1195 |           item.validity = status;
1196 |           this.save(type, item);
1197 |         }
1198 |   }
1199 |   invalidateProfileDependents(uid) {
1200 |     const facts = this.profile(uid).facts;
1201 |     const validDependencies = (item) =>
1202 |       (item.dependencies || []).every((dep) => {
1203 |         if (dep.fact_id)
1204 |           return facts.some(
1205 |             (f) => f.fact_id === dep.fact_id && f.revision === dep.revision && f.field === "instruction",
1206 |           );
1207 |         return this.all("source", uid).some(
1208 |           (source) => source.id === dep.source_id && source.text_version === dep.source_text_version,
1209 |         );
1210 |       });
1211 |     // Visits depend on the whole profile. Care remains usable only with unchanged
1212 |     // instruction/source dependencies; previously stale tasks are never revived.
1213 |     this.invalidate(uid, (item) => item.kind === "visit_preparation" || !validDependencies(item), "stale");
1214 |   }
1215 |   stageResult(run, args, read) {
1216 |     keys(args, ["kind", "payload", "dependencies", "operation_key"]);
1217 |     return this.receipt(run.owner, `result:${run.id}`, args.operation_key, args, () => {
1218 |       check(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(args)) <= this.limits.outputBytes, "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
1219 |       check(
1220 |         (run.workflow === "visit" && args.kind === "visit_preparation") ||
1221 |           (run.workflow === "chat" && args.kind === "assistant_reply") ||
1222 |           (run.workflow === "care_plan" && args.kind === "care_plan"),
1223 |         "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT",
1224 |         422,
1225 |       );
1226 |       check(this.user(run.owner).revision === run.base_profile_revision, "REVISION_CONFLICT", 409);
1227 |       check(Array.isArray(args.dependencies) && args.dependencies.length <= 100, "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
1228 |       const facts = this.profile(run.owner).facts;
1229 |       const seenTools = this.all("tool_receipt", run.owner)
1230 |         .filter((x) => x.run_id === run.id)
1231 |         .map((x) => x.tool);
1232 |       check(
1233 |         ["visit", "chat"].includes(run.workflow)
1234 |           ? seenTools.includes("healththread_read_source") &&
1235 |               seenTools.includes("healththread_retrieve_context")
1236 |           : seenTools.includes("healththread_get_confirmed_instructions"),
1237 |         "TOOL_SEQUENCE_REQUIRED",
1238 |         422,
1239 |       );
1240 |       const factRef = (ref) => {
1241 |         keys(ref, ["fact_id", "revision"]);
1242 |         const f = facts.find((f) => f.fact_id === ref.fact_id && f.revision === ref.revision);
1243 |         check(
1244 |           f && run.read_facts?.some((r) => r.fact_id === ref.fact_id && r.revision === ref.revision),
1245 |           "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT",
1246 |           422,
1247 |         );
1248 |         return f;
1249 |       };
1250 |       for (const dep of args.dependencies) {
1251 |         if (dep.fact_id) factRef(dep);
1252 |         else {
1253 |           keys(dep, ["source_id", "source_text_version"]);
1254 |           check(
1255 |             run.read_sources?.includes(dep.source_id) &&
1256 |               read(run, dep.source_id).text_version === dep.source_text_version,
1257 |             "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT",
1258 |             422,
1259 |           );
1260 |         }
1261 |       }
1262 |       const hasFact = (ref) =>
1263 |         args.dependencies.some((d) => d.fact_id === ref.fact_id && d.revision === ref.revision);
1264 |       const payload = structuredClone(args.payload);
1265 |       if (["visit_preparation", "assistant_reply"].includes(args.kind)) {
1266 |         const chat = args.kind === "assistant_reply";
1267 |         if (chat) check(["greeting", "clarify", "prepare", "records", "scope", "offer_visit", "confirm_visit"].includes(payload.intent), "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT", 422);
1268 |         if(chat && payload.intent==='confirm_visit')this.validateVisitConfirmation(run);
1269 |         keys(
1270 |           payload,
1271 |           ["speech", "questions", "unknowns", "claims", ...(chat ? ["intent"] : [])],
1272 |           ["speech", "questions", "unknowns", "claims"],
1273 |         );
1274 |         check(
1275 |           typeof payload.speech === "string" &&
1276 |             payload.speech.length <= 20000 &&
1277 |             Array.isArray(payload.questions) &&
1278 |             payload.questions.every((x) => typeof x === "string" && x.length <= 1000) &&
1279 |             payload.questions.length <= 20 &&
1280 |             Array.isArray(payload.unknowns) &&
1281 |             payload.unknowns.every((x) => typeof x === "string" && x.length <= 1000) &&
1282 |             payload.unknowns.length <= 30 &&
1283 |             Array.isArray(payload.claims) &&
1284 |             payload.claims.length <= 100,
1285 |           "INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT",
1286 |           422,
1287 |         );

1634 |   deleteData(uid, sourceId, key, expectedEpoch) {
1635 |     return this.transaction(() =>
1636 |       this.receipt(uid, `delete:${sourceId}`, key, { source_id: sourceId }, () => {
1637 |         this.checkExpectedEpoch(uid, expectedEpoch);
1638 |         this.user(uid);
1639 |         if (sourceId !== "all") this.get(uid, "source", sourceId);
1640 |         const sourceIds = sourceId === "all" ? this.all("source", uid).map((s) => s.id) : [sourceId];

1642 |         let derivedAdded=true;
1643 |         while(derivedAdded){derivedAdded=false;for(const source of this.all('source',uid))if(!sourceIds.includes(source.id)&&source.derived_source_ids?.some(sid=>sourceIds.includes(sid))){sourceIds.push(source.id);derivedAdded=true;}}

1650 |         for (const r of this.all("run", uid))
1651 |           if (active.includes(r.status)) {
1652 |             r.status = "cancelled";
1653 |             r.phase = "deleted";
1654 |             r.scope_epoch++;
1655 |             this.save("run", r);
1656 |             this.finishDispatch(r);
1657 |           }
1658 |         const related = (obj) =>
1659 |           sourceId === "all" ||
1660 |           sourceIds.includes(obj.id) ||
1661 |           sourceIds.includes(obj.source_id) ||
1662 |           obj.source_ids?.some((s) => sourceIds.includes(s)) ||
1663 |           obj.evidence?.some((e) => sourceIds.includes(e.source_id));
1664 |         this.expireCheckpoints(uid, "source_scope_changed");
1665 |         const removedFacts = new Set(
1666 |           this.all("fact_revision", uid)
1667 |             .filter(related)
1668 |             .map((f) => f.fact_id),

1670 |         const types = [
1671 |           "record_thread","record_summary","record_confirmation",
1672 |           "runtime_binding",
1673 |           "fact_revision",
1674 |           "fact_change",
1675 |           "result",

1677 |           "entry",
1678 |           "document",
1679 |           "episode",
1680 |           "source",
1681 |         ];
1682 |         const objects = types.flatMap((type) => this.all(type, uid));
1683 |         const removed = new Set();
1684 |         let expanded = true;
1685 |         while (expanded) {
1686 |           expanded = false;
1687 |           for (const obj of objects) {
1688 |             if (removed.has(obj.id)) continue;
1689 |             const dependent =
1690 |               related(obj) ||
1691 |               removedFacts.has(obj.fact_id) ||
1692 |               obj.dependencies?.some((d) => removedFacts.has(d.fact_id) || sourceIds.includes(d.source_id)) ||
1693 |               obj.proposals?.some(
1694 |                 (p) =>
1695 |                   removedFacts.has(p.fact_id) || p.evidence?.some((e) => sourceIds.includes(e.source_id)),
1696 |               ) ||
1697 |               removed.has(obj.result_id) ||
1698 |               removed.has(obj.record_thread_id) ||
1699 |               removed.has(obj.fact_change_id);
1700 |             if (dependent) {
1701 |               removed.add(obj.id);
1702 |               expanded = true;
1703 |             }
1704 |           }
1705 |         }
1706 |         for (const objectId of removed) {
1707 |           this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM objects WHERE id=? AND owner=?").run(objectId, uid);
1708 |           this.db.prepare("UPDATE receipts SET outcome='{}' WHERE owner=? AND operation=?").run(uid,`edit:${objectId}`);
1709 |         }

1712 |         for (const run of this.all("run", uid)) {
1713 |           const direct = related(run),
1714 |             indirect =
1715 |               removed.has(run.result_id) || removed.has(run.result_ref) || removed.has(run.fact_change_id);
1716 |           if (!direct && !indirect) continue;
1717 |           run.fact_change_id = null;
1718 |           run.result_id = null;
1719 |           run.result_ref = null;
1720 |           run.scope_epoch++;
1721 |           run.status = "cancelled";
1722 |           run.phase = direct ? "deleted" : "result_withdrawn";
1723 |           run.runtime = run.runtime ? { mode: run.runtime.mode, trace_erasure: "unverified" } : undefined;
1724 |           if (direct) {
1725 |             run.source_ids = [];
1726 |             run.document_id = null;
1727 |             run.deleted = true;
1728 |           }

1730 |           this.save("run", run);

1732 |         }

1744 |               if (removed.has(op[field])) op[field] = null;
1745 |             this.save("operation", op);
1746 |           }

1751 |             sqlite: { status: "deleted", sources: sourceIds.length },

   2 | const PUBLIC_CODES=new Set(['AGENT_NOT_CONFIGURED','AGNES_PROVIDER_REQUIRED','DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE','DEADLINE_EXCEEDED','MISSING_STAGED_OUTPUT','INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT','TOOL_SEQUENCE_REQUIRED','REVISION_CONFLICT','SCOPE_REVOKED','RUN_CANCELLED','AGH_NOT_READY','OUTPUT_REPAIR_EXHAUSTED']);
   3 | export class DispatchQueue {
   4 |  constructor(store,agent=null,{limits={},onDiagnostic=event=>console.error(JSON.stringify(event))}={}){
   5 |   this.store=store;this.agent=agent;this.limits={...LIMITS,...limits};this.onDiagnostic=onDiagnostic;
   6 |   this.running=new Map();this.pendingFailures=new Set();this.closed=false;
   7 |   this.dispatchFailures=0;this.nextAttemptAt=0;this.suspended=false;this.lastError=null;
   8 |   this.timer=setInterval(()=>this.kick(),250);this.timer.unref();this.kick();
   9 |  }
  10 |  status(){
  11 |   return {state:this.suspended?'suspended':this.dispatchFailures?'backoff':'ready',suspended:this.suspended,consecutive_failures:this.dispatchFailures,next_attempt_at:this.nextAttemptAt||null,last_error:this.lastError?{...this.lastError}:null};
  12 |  }
  13 |  recordFailure(error,phase,runId=null){
  14 |   this.dispatchFailures++;
  15 |   this.suspended=this.dispatchFailures>=this.limits.dispatchMaxAttempts;
  16 |   const delay=Math.min(this.limits.dispatchBackoffMaxMs,this.limits.dispatchBackoffMs*2**Math.min(this.dispatchFailures-1,20));
  17 |   this.nextAttemptAt=this.suspended?0:this.store.clock()+delay;
  18 |   const code=String(error?.code||'').startsWith('ERR_SQLITE')?'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE':'DISPATCH_UNAVAILABLE';
  19 |   this.lastError={...errorBody(new DomainError(503,code,code),phase).error,retryable:!this.suspended};
  20 |   // Never log SQL, error.message, credentials, paths or source text. Logging cannot escape the scheduler.
  21 |   try{this.onDiagnostic({...this.lastError,run_id:runId,attempt:this.dispatchFailures,next_attempt_at:this.nextAttemptAt||null,suspended:this.suspended});}catch{}
  22 |  }
  23 |  settleUnrecordedFailures(){
  24 |   for(const runId of this.pendingFailures){
  25 |    const current=this.store.internalRun(runId);
  26 |    if(['needs_review','completed'].includes(current.status)){
  27 |     this.store.transaction(()=>{const latest=this.store.internalRun(runId);latest.execution_error=errorBody(new DomainError(503,'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE','STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE'),'after_checkpoint').error;this.store.save('run',latest);});
  28 |    }else this.store.failRun(runId,'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE','interrupted');
  29 |    this.pendingFailures.delete(runId);
  30 |   }
  31 |  }
  32 |  kick(){
  33 |   if(this.closed||this.suspended||this.store.clock()<this.nextAttemptAt)return;
  34 |   try{
  35 |    this.settleUnrecordedFailures();
  36 |    const pending=this.store.all('dispatch').filter(x=>x.state==='queued');
  37 |    for(const dispatch of pending){
  38 |     if(this.running.size>=this.limits.concurrency)break;
  39 |     if([...this.running.values()].filter(task=>task.owner===dispatch.owner).length>=this.limits.userConcurrency)continue;
  40 |     const run=this.store.claimRun(dispatch.run_id);if(!run)continue;
  41 |     const controller=new AbortController(),task={owner:run.owner,controller,promise:null};
  42 |     this.running.set(run.id,task);
  43 |     task.promise=this.execute(run,controller).catch(error=>{
  44 |      controller.abort();this.pendingFailures.add(run.id);this.recordFailure(error,'dispatch_execution',run.id);
  45 |     }).finally(()=>{this.running.delete(run.id);if(!this.closed)queueMicrotask(()=>this.kick());});
  46 |    }
  47 |    this.dispatchFailures=0;this.nextAttemptAt=0;this.lastError=null;
  48 |   }catch(error){this.recordFailure(error,'dispatch_claim');}
  49 |  }
  50 |  // Explicit operator recovery after the finite retry budget; never replays an unknown remote effect.
  51 |  resume(){if(!this.closed){this.suspended=false;this.dispatchFailures=0;this.nextAttemptAt=0;this.kick();}return this.status();}
  52 |  async execute(run,controller){let timeout,abortHandler;try{
  53 |   if(!this.agent||!['fixture','real'].includes(this.agent.mode))throw new DomainError(503,'AGENT_NOT_CONFIGURED');
  54 |   const deadline=Math.max(1,Math.min(this.limits.deadlineMs,run.deadline-this.store.clock()));
  55 |   const execute=Promise.resolve().then(async()=>{if(this.agent.isReady&&!(await this.agent.isReady({signal:controller.signal})))throw new DomainError(503,'AGH_NOT_READY');return this.agent.run({run,scope:{run_id:run.id,allowed_source_ids:run.source_ids,snapshot_revision:run.base_profile_revision,consent_version:run.consent_version,expires_at:run.expires_at},tools:this.boundTools(run.id,controller.signal),signal:controller.signal});});
  56 |   const expired=new Promise((_,reject)=>{timeout=setTimeout(()=>{reject(new DomainError(504,'DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'));controller.abort();},deadline);});
  57 |   const aborted=new Promise((_,reject)=>{abortHandler=()=>reject(new DomainError(409,'RUN_CANCELLED'));if(controller.signal.aborted)abortHandler();else controller.signal.addEventListener('abort',abortHandler,{once:true});});
  58 |   const output=await Promise.race([execute,expired,aborted]);const current=this.store.internalRun(run.id);
  59 |   if(!['needs_review','completed'].includes(current.status))throw new DomainError(422,'MISSING_STAGED_OUTPUT');
  60 |   let metadata={mode:'fixture'};
  61 |   if(this.agent.mode==='real'){
  62 |    const agh=output?.agh;if(!agh||agh.provider!=='agnes-ai'||typeof agh.session_id!=='string'||!agh.session_id||!Number.isSafeInteger(agh.last_seq)||agh.last_seq<0||typeof agh.model!=='string')throw new DomainError(422,'INVALID_AGENT_OUTPUT');

  65 |   }
  66 |   this.store.setRuntime(run.id,metadata);
  67 |  }catch(error){const code=PUBLIC_CODES.has(error.code)?error.code:'DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE';const current=this.store.internalRun(run.id);
  68 |   if(['needs_review','completed'].includes(current.status)){this.store.transaction(()=>{const latest=this.store.internalRun(run.id);latest.execution_error=errorBody(new DomainError(503,code,code),'after_checkpoint').error;this.store.save('run',latest);});}
  69 |   else this.store.failRun(run.id,code,['AGENT_NOT_CONFIGURED','AGH_NOT_READY','AGNES_PROVIDER_REQUIRED'].includes(code)?'blocked':'failed');
  70 |  }finally{clearTimeout(timeout);if(abortHandler)controller.signal.removeEventListener('abort',abortHandler);}}
  71 |  boundTools(runId,signal){
  72 |   return Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(this.store.toolsFor(runId)).map(([name,call])=>[name,(args,invocation)=>{
  73 |    if(signal.aborted)throw new DomainError(409,'RUN_CANCELLED');
  74 |    return invocation?this.store.toolsFor(runId,invocation)[name](args):call(args);
  75 |   }])));
  76 |  }
  77 |  cancel(runId){this.running.get(runId)?.controller.abort();}
  78 |  async drain(){this.kick();while(this.running.size){await Promise.allSettled([...this.running.values()].map(x=>x.promise));this.kick();}}
  79 |  async close(){this.closed=true;clearInterval(this.timer);for(const [rid,t]of this.running){try{this.store.failRun(rid,'RUN_INTERRUPTED','interrupted');}catch(error){this.recordFailure(error,'dispatch_close',rid);}finally{t.controller.abort();}}await Promise.allSettled([...this.running.values()].map(t=>t.promise));if(this.agent?.close)await this.agent.close();}

   1 | import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
   2 | export class DomainError extends Error {
   3 |  constructor(status,code,message,details){super(message);Object.assign(this,{status,code,details});}
   4 | }
   5 | export function fail(status,code,message=code,details){throw new DomainError(status,code,message,details);}
   6 | export function check(condition,code='VALIDATION_ERROR',status=400){if(!condition)fail(status,code);}
   7 | export function errorBody(error,phase='request',requestId=randomUUID()) {return {error:{code:error.status?error.code:'INTERNAL_ERROR',message:error.status?error.message:'服务暂时不可用。',request_id:requestId,phase,retryable:['QUEUE_BUSY','DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE'].includes(error.code),...(error.details?{details:error.details}:{})}};}

 199 |   async wait(id,options={}) {
 200 |     const cancel=()=>this.client.cancelRun(id).catch(()=>{});options.signal?.addEventListener('abort',cancel,{once:true});
 201 |     try {const run=await this.client.pollRun(id,{requireRuntime:this.client.state.capabilities?.analysis?.mode==='real',...options});await this.refresh();

 203 |       return run;
 204 |     }finally{options.signal?.removeEventListener('abort',cancel);}

 206 |   pendingChange(sourceId) {

   1 | # 用 Agnes Harness 完成 HealthThread 后端
   2 | 
   3 | 本提示词包依据上传 PRD 和当前已运行的双端仓库制作，基线 HealthThread commit 为 `744faa918f1525296194af2842ba0f8d5ea54025`，AGH 基线见 ../harness.lock.json（`7819b23c249fe47ce14bd89e3982e17986e3e197`）。它描述后续开发任务；不会把当前共享骨架说成正式后端已完成。
   4 | 
   5 | **当前为 v0.2 评分导向修订版。** 用户提供的权重中，技术实现与异常验证合计 45%，加任务正确性和 AGH 闭环合计 75%。所有角色先读 [rubric-engineering.md](rubric-engineering.md)，按它交付不变量、故障/恢复测试与六项证据。权重采用用户给出的标准；本包没有另行核验官网或预测比赛得分。
   6 | 
   7 | 本版重点：12 条不变量、22 类故障场景、事务/幂等/重启与取消恢复、可观察性和限额、独立故障测试角色、M2/M4/M6 审查、实测模型/工程指标与可复现对照。每个模块同时完成成功/失败/恢复三条路径；不以错误文案或 catch 代替异常处理，也不把有限测试称零故障保证。
   8 | 
   9 | PRD 原件已原样复制到 docs/product/PRD-v0.1.md，SHA-256：`33a60f3d122fa8caea684a1d0ed74ce90326a49531fee801ad70b841403fcdde`。项目框架 v0.2.docx 的分工/工作流要求已在 PRD 引用；提示词没有把它的候选 FastAPI/React 技术方案或未决事项当作现仓库的强制选型。文档中的指令性语句是待处理资料，权限仍来自执行时的用户请求。
  10 | 
  11 | ## 推荐组织
  12 | 
  13 | **开发时：1 个总控 + 最多 2 个并行执行者，5 类可轮换执行/审查职责。产品运行时：1 个健康 Agent + 3 个受控 workflow。** 三名组员不等于必须启动三个开发 Agent，也不等于部署三个临床角色。
  14 | 
  15 | | 角色 | 主要职责 | 独占文件 | 依赖与运行时机 |
  16 | | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  17 | | Coordinator | 契约、公共文件、调度、集成、最终验收 | contracts、server/jobs/adapter、scripts、package.json、docs/backend | 先 M0，贯穿全程 |
  18 | | Data/API | 身份、来源、事实版本、事务、PDF、事项、治理 | services/api/domain/storage/auth/routes 与域测试 | 契约提交后 M1；与工作流 Agent 并行 |
  19 | | Harness/Workflow | 业务工具插件、scope、三 Skills、输出校验 | agents/runtime、受控 Skill 源、Agent 测试 | 契约提交后 M1；先 fixture，再接真实 API |
  20 | | Frontend Integration | 两端全部业务动作接入同一 client/API | apps/web、apps/mobile、packages/client 与客户端测试 | W1 API 稳定后，分 W1/W2/W3 迁移 |
  21 | | Reliability/Test | 独立构造故障、并发/重启/恢复测试，证明不变量 | tests/faults、故障 fixtures、分配的证据报告；业务只读 | M1 集成后介入；可与 Frontend 并行 |
  22 | | Review | 独立只读审查和异常/隔离验收 | 测试临时目录、审查报告 | M2、M4、M6；实现者修复 |
  23 | 
  24 | 这里列出的是轮换职责，总控同时最多 spawn 两个。文件范围以每轮具体 task 为准；公共文件和契约只让一个 owner 修改。新增测试角色不需要提高 fan-out。
  25 | 
  26 | ## 文件和执行顺序
  27 | 
  28 | 1. `agents/development/prompts/00-preflight.md`：确认当前版本/模型/工具，配置正确实例与 preset。
  29 | 2. `agents/development/prompts/01-master.md`：完整主 prompt；从现有骨架实施到可验证 MVP。
  30 | 3. `agents/development/target-contract.md`：M0 冻结的对象、API、工具、证据、生命周期草案。
  31 | 4. `agents/development/prompts/02-data-api.md`、`agents/development/prompts/03-harness-workflows.md`：M1 可并行的任务正文。
  32 | 5. `agents/development/prompts/04-frontend-integration.md`：两端渐进接入，共享适配层一个 owner。
  33 | 6. `agents/development/prompts/05-review.md`：独立验收；只读。
  34 | 7. `agents/development/prompts/06-resume.md`：阶段中断后接续，先核对证据，不推倒重来。
  35 | 8. `agents/development/rubric-engineering.md`：评分到代码/证据、不变量、故障矩阵、工程指标与硬门槛，所有角色必读。
  36 | 9. `agents/development/prompts/07-reliability-tests.md`：专门的故障/恢复测试角色，业务和契约只读。
  37 | 
  38 | 建议在 AGH TUI/Web 中开启一个**开发会话**：先发送 00，确认实际环境结果，再发送 01。总控自行读取角色 prompt 并 spawn，不需要人工分别启动所有角色。
  39 | 
  40 | 直接粘贴以下短启动指令也可以，完整要求由文件承载：
  41 | 
  42 | ```text
  43 | 请在当前 HealthThread 仓库执行 agents/development/prompts/00-preflight.md。
  44 | 完成可运行的预检与配置接线后，读取并完整执行
  45 | agents/development/prompts/01-master.md 和 agents/development/target-contract.md。
  46 | 先读取 agents/development/rubric-engineering.md，按 v0.2 的 I01–I12、E01–E22 验收。
  47 | 使用 AGH 原生 subagent_spawn/collect/cancel；最多两个执行者并行，
  48 | 先冻结契约，再实现 W1、W2、W3，逐阶段接入手机与电脑前端。
  49 | 常规实现、测试、本地提交可继续；尚无外部 push/部署授权。
  50 | 如真实模型或插件信任阻塞，完成其他工程部分并保存准确缺口。
  51 | ```
  52 | 
  53 | 如果 AGH 已用旧版 prompt 开始工作，在同一个开发会话发送：
  54 | 
  55 | ```text
  56 | 继续现有 HealthThread 项目，采用本地 agents/development 的 v0.2 提示词。
  57 | 先读 agents/development/rubric-engineering.md 和更新后的 agents/development/prompts/01-master.md，再执行 agents/development/prompts/06-resume.md。
  58 | 保留已经完成的代码与提交，核对当前阶段的 I01–I12 和适用 E01–E22。
  59 | 调度 agents/development/prompts/07-reliability-tests.md 补独立故障/恢复测试；M2/M4/M6 复核。
  60 | 优先修复阻断不变量的问题，再继续未完成模块。不要重新初始化项目。
  61 | ```
  62 | 
  63 | 对于不能读本地文件的界面，先将 PRD、当前仓库与本包加入实际 AGH workspace，或把 01 与 target-contract 全文一起提供；只给仓库 URL 不能假定模型已经读到私有代码。子 Agent 的上下文也必须可读，不能只分派“帮我写后端”。
  64 | 
  65 | ### 从 CLI 发送文件
  66 | 
  67 | 下面两条路径已对齐这台电脑的桌面副本和现有 AGH 源码；AGH_HOME 保持与**开发实例**一致。换机器时重新核验路径；不要把模板占位符当作实际目录。路径规则见 [PATHS.md](PATHS.md)。以下是启动示例，不会在阅读此文时自动运行：
  68 | 
  69 | ```sh
  70 | HT_REPO='/Users/fancyouth123/Desktop/HealthThread-Backend-Dev'
  71 | AGH_ROOT='/Users/fancyouth123/Documents/Codex/2026-10-06/https-github-com-agnesai-labs-agnes/work/agnes-harness'
  72 | # 沿用已配置的 AGH_HOME；未设置时保留 AGH 默认值，不要直接使用占位目录。
  73 | 
  74 | # 优先用 TUI，以便处理真实工具审批。
  75 | node "$AGH_ROOT/packages/cli/dist/local/agnes.mjs" \
  76 |   --profile local-dev --preset standard --cwd "$HT_REPO" \
  77 |   '读取并执行 agents/development/prompts/00-preflight.md，然后按实际环境接续 agents/development/prompts/01-master.md。'
  78 | 
  79 | # 适合已解决审批的非交互任务：stdin 直接作为 prompt。
  80 | node "$AGH_ROOT/packages/cli/dist/local/agnes.mjs" \
  81 |   -p --profile local-dev --preset standard --cwd "$HT_REPO" \
  82 |   < "$HT_REPO/agents/development/prompts/00-preflight.md"
  83 | ```
  84 | 
  85 | 若 00 已经证明自定义 recipe 真实装载，将后续会话的 `--preset standard` 换成 `--preset healththread-dev`。若使用 `--connect`，选实际开发连接目标，不要给上面命令随意追加未知 daemon 参数。Print 模式处理审批能力有限；被拒绝时回到同一配置的交互式会话，不能通过关闭审批绕过。
  86 | 
  87 | 续跑可以在同一 TUI 粘贴 06。另开 CLI 时用实际 `agh resume SESSION_ID -p ...`，SESSION_ID 从真实 sessions 结果获得；不要猜 `--session` 参数或伪造子任务 ID。[官方 CLI 参考](https://github.com/AgnesAI-Labs/agnes-harness/blob/main/docs/reference/cli.md)。
  88 | 
  89 | ## multi-agent 设置：真正的字段与装载方式
  90 | 
  91 | 两份 JSON 是 **AGH PresetDoc 定义模板**，不包含模型密钥，不是自动生效的项目配置：
  92 | 
  93 | - `presets/healththread-dev.json`：继承 standard，max_depth=1、max_fan_out=2、worktree、aggregate budget、每轮最多 36 模型步骤，关闭自动改写 harness 内容。
  94 | - `presets/healththread-runtime.json`：SDK 产品会话，子任务深度/并发=0、core 工具为空、每轮最多 12 模型步骤。**这只是收紧的起点**，仍要过滤最终扩展工具、设置健康角色和 scope，不能独立作为安全保证或可运行工作流。
  95 | 
  96 | 36/12 步、超时和输出 allowance 是建议起始值，不是 PRD 已冻结的 D09，也不是费用额度。根据真实轨迹调整，超限时明确状态，不悄悄取消上限。输出修复最多 2 次由业务工作流另行控制，不等于 12 个总步骤。
  97 | 
  98 | 继承解析还会带入 standard 的 per_request_cap=4000；固定版子任务 factory 在未指定 tree_budget_credits 时使用 20 credits 树额度。它们是当前源码默认值，不是本包选择的比赛费用预算，也不代表两名 Agent 足够完成整段工作；00 必须核验实际报价/账户，再把每轮任务切小或填写已确认的树额度。产品模板同时将 Harness repair.max_rounds 收紧为 2，但业务侧仍须独立记录修复次数，避免两层各自重复重试。
  99 | 
 100 | 核心片段如下（属于 preset）：
 101 | 
 102 | ```json
 103 | {
 104 |   "name": "healththread-dev",
 105 |   "extends": "standard",
 106 |   "subagent": {
 107 |     "max_depth": 1,
 108 |     "max_fan_out": 2,
 109 |     "isolation": "worktree",
 110 |     "budget_inherit": "aggregate"
 111 |   },
 112 |   "budget": { "max_steps": 36, "on_exceed": "deny" }
 113 | }
 114 | ```
 115 | 
 116 | 当前固定版本 @agnes/code 仅加载已登记 PRESET_NAMES。单独把 JSON 放进本项目、`.agh/presets` 或 profile 不会提供新 preset。00 已给出最小注册任务：优先使用当前版本有证据的公开加载路径；固定版本可在团队自己的 AGH 分支新增 recipe/登记名/构建 CLI，更新真实版本锁。它改产品 recipe，不要求改 Core；冻结 minimal-rl 不动。
 117 | 
 118 | profile **仅选择已提供的 preset**。注册完成后，把以下片段合并到对应实例的 `AGH_HOME/profiles/local-dev/profile.yaml`，不要整份覆盖其他配置：
 119 | 
 120 | ```yaml
 121 | # 开发 AGH_HOME 的 profile 层
 122 | presets:
 123 |   default: healththread-dev
 124 |   allowed: [standard, healththread-dev]
 125 | ```
 126 | 
 127 | 产品实例另用一个 AGH_HOME：
 128 | 
 129 | ```yaml
 130 | # 产品 AGH_HOME 的 profile 层；产品业务 SDK 选该实例
 131 | presets:
 132 |   default: healththread-runtime
 133 |   allowed: [healththread-runtime]
 134 | computerUse:
 135 |   enabled: false
 136 | ```
 137 | 
 138 | profile 层允许名不会凭空装载 definition；运行 daemon 还需使用装配了新 preset 的构建。必须验证“schema 合法→继承解析→实际实例目录与路由→拒绝路径”，不能跳到“限制已生效”。[官方配置参考](https://github.com/AgnesAI-Labs/agnes-harness/blob/main/docs/reference/configuration.md)。
 139 | 
 140 | 现阶段可直接从内置 standard 开始，总控严格只派两个；standard 的默认 fan-out=4 是实际硬上限。若不能修改/部署 AGH recipe，清楚保留这个区别，采用顺序执行或软调度限制，不伪称配置已更改。
 141 | 
 142 | ### 模型设置
 143 | 
 144 | 首先沿用你已经能调用成功的 Agnes 模型，减少同时更换模型和工作流的变量。模型名称、route、thinking 支持以实际 catalog 为准。不要虚构 model_id，route 也可能是 account-...。
 145 | 
 146 | 检查五个文本槽：primary、escalation、fast、compaction、verifier 都解析为 Agnes。首轮可使用同一个 Agnes 模型；稳定后才按实际能力/预算区分。SDK 当前真实形式为：
 147 | 
 148 | ```js
 149 | for (const slot of ['primary', 'escalation', 'fast', 'compaction', 'verifier']) {
 150 |   await session.setModel({ slot, route: actualAgnesRoute, model: actualAgnesModel });
 151 | }
 152 | ```
 153 | 
 154 | 以上是接线代码片段，变量须来自已核验配置。CLI 里的多次 --model 在固定 parser 中不是五个槽的批量设置方案，不能凭空列五个参数就宣称全部固定。实际设置/检查由 00 和 SDK adapter 完成；压缩/验证/嵌入/语音同样不能偷偷切别家模型。
 155 | 
 156 | ### 真正的子任务调用
 157 | 
 158 | 以下是工具调用参数示例，不是 JSON 配置文件；task 应替换为角色正文与明确交接信息：
 159 | 
 160 | ```json
 161 | {
 162 |   "task": "执行 agents/development/prompts/02-data-api.md。基线=<已提交SHA>，契约=<版本>。仅改 services/api/domain/storage/auth/routes 与 tests/domain。交付 source/context/stage 端口及确认事务。公共 server/client/contract 只读。跑给定测试，本地提交，回报 SHA/接口/结果/限制。",
 163 |   "isolation": "worktree"
 164 | }
 165 | ```
 166 | 
 167 | ```json
 168 | {
 169 |   "task": "执行 agents/development/prompts/03-harness-workflows.md。使用同一基线/契约，仅改 agents/runtime、指定产品 Skill 源及 tests/agent。用冻结内部端口 fixture 实现五个健康工具和三工作流。公共 server/adapter/contract 只读。测试、本地提交，回报接线建议。",
 170 |   "isolation": "worktree"
 171 | }
 172 | ```
 173 | 
 174 | 两次分别调用 `subagent_spawn`，记住实际返回 `details.childKey` 和 `details.isolation/worktree`；再调用：
 175 | 
 176 | ```json
 177 | { "childKey": "<真实返回的childKey>", "wait": true }
 178 | ```
 179 | 
 180 | 工具名是 `subagent_collect`。取消用 `subagent_cancel({childKey})` 后 collect 确认终态。`model` 可选：验证槽仍是 Agnes 后才能用 "fast"/"verifier"；不填则继承。`budget` 是正整数 credits 上限，不是 tokens；没有已确认费用数值时省略并说明继承的实际树额度，不能写成“无限预算”。
 181 | 
 182 | worktree 从已提交 HEAD 起步：角色 prompt/PRD/contract/fixtures 要在基线中，不能只存在总控未提交文件。实际退回 shared 时串行写代码。collect 不 cherry-pick/merge，不证明改动已经接进主程序；总控检查 diff/commit/tests 后集成。工具字段和行为以 [固定版 subagent 源码](https://github.com/AgnesAI-Labs/agnes-harness/blob/7819b23c249fe47ce14bd89e3982e17986e3e197/packages/base/extensions/subagent/src/tools.ts) 为准。
 183 | 
 184 | ## 你们三个人怎么配合
 185 | 
 186 | - 你：AGH 预检、工具/Skill、总控集成与真实模型轨迹。优先盯住权限、来源校验和模型失败恢复。
 187 | - 数据/接口同学：复核 Data/API 的 schema、确认事务、身份、授权与删除；接口冻结时与你一起核对。
 188 | - 前端/视觉同学：复核两端的核对、来源、过期、错误文案与用户动作；接入由 Frontend Agent 沿用现有代码，不要求重新出一套页面。
 189 | 
 190 | 这是建议的人类复核分工，不冒充团队已承诺的人员职责或医疗审阅。关键产品待决仍回写 PRD；专业规则不能由软件 Review Agent 背书。
 191 | 
 192 | ## 实施 pipeline 和验收
 193 | 
 194 | | 阶段 | 看得到的结果 | 过关证据 |
 195 | | --- | --- | --- |
 196 | | M0 | 已冻结 API/schema、页面动作表、实际 AGH 预检 | 基线重跑；契约 fixture；真实 preset/模型槽检查 |
 197 | | M1 | 身份/原文/版本/受限业务工具可连接 | 原子接收/持久化派发、权限/来源/事务与未知结果；包与 Skill 状态 |
 198 | | M2 W1 | 手机记录→电脑核对→手机有效新版本 | 原文先保存、真实三步工具、故障/恢复、确认事务、冲突/晚返回；第一次独立复核 |
 199 | | M3 W2 | 有效历史生成表达/问题/来源；更正旧卡过期 | 来源支持、版本依赖；D01/D02 明确关闭 |
 200 | | M4 W3 | 真实文本 PDF→差异核对→多医嘱事项 | PDF 定位/资源边界、确认接续、多事项故障；第二次独立复核 |
 201 | | M5 | 两端每个动作统一 API，导出/删除有范围 | 实际双 UI 演示、两账号隔离、草稿/删除/过期状态 |
 202 | | M6 | 可复现 MVP 与真实 Agnes 证据 | 不变量/故障矩阵、12+12 模型案例、对照/实测指标；最终 review、真实轨迹与六项证据 |
 203 | 
 204 | 自动测试入口必须包含新增嵌套域测试；当前 `node --test tests/*.test.mjs` 不会自动发现 tests/domain/agent/faults。M0/总控更新 npm test 统一入口后检查实际执行数量，不能新建测试却让 CI 根本没跑。模型的 12+12 案例不是故障矩阵；后者要验证 HTTP/run/UI/DB 和恢复。
 205 | 
 206 | 工程硬门槛：越权、Agent 自行写正式事实、错误事实/医嘱生效、重复提交、删除后恢复资料、伪真轨迹，任一可复现即阻断 MVP 验收；其他项完成不抵消。score-evidence 按六项指向实际代码/测试/运行，不能填自评高分。详细矩阵与指标见 agents/development/rubric-engineering.md。
 207 | 
 208 | 停在契约、假路由、fixture 或“一次生成 JSON”都不算最终闭环。是否完成分别报告 implemented/tested/real_model/not_configured。D01/D02/语音未决可与核心演示分开，但不能称完整 PRD 全部验收或正式真实患者可用。
 209 | 
 210 | ## 本包验证范围
 211 | 
 212 | 本包仅编写 prompt、目标契约和 preset 模板，并复制 PRD；不改变当前产品运行代码、安装 AGH 插件或消耗模型额度。模板需经实际装配验证后才能选择；生成本包不等于后端已实现。核验记录见 validation.md。

  18 | 
  19 | 1. 记录 Node 版本、AGH 版本、profile、实际连接目标、工具可用性。执行当前版本真实支持的只读 doctor/profile inspect；doctor subagents 仅列举子任务维护状态，不证明工具注册成功。不要自动调用 --repair。
  20 | 2. 在实际会话工具目录查找 subagent_spawn、subagent_collect、subagent_cancel，说明它们是否能用。默认 index.ts 的空入口不能单独证明功能缺失；检查 Host 的 ecosystem 装配路径。工具的真实字段只有 task、model、isolation、budget，collect 用 childKey、wait。
  21 | 3. 核对 Agnes 模型目录和配置的 route。所有 primary/escalation/fast/compaction/verifier 槽必须解析到允许的 Agnes 模型。route 可能为 account-...，不能硬编码为 provider 名。不要输出 API key、secret 引用的内容或完整环境变量。
  22 | 4. 立即可用方案：使用内置 standard，主 prompt 控制同时最多两个子任务；说明 standard 默认 max_fan_out=4，此时“最多两个”只是调度约束。不要声称硬限制已经变成 2。
  23 | 5. 如需要硬限制，加载 healththread-dev 的目标 preset。当前固定源码的 @agnes/code 只导出 PRESET_NAMES 中的 recipe，项目中放 JSON 或 .agh/presets 文件不会自动注册。先检查当前版本是否已有公开的自定义 preset 装载方式，有则按该方式接入并验证；没有时，在团队自己的 AGH checkout/分支中做最小 recipe 注册修改：
  24 |    - 将 healththread-dev.json 与 healththread-runtime.json 转为相应的 packages/code/presets/<name>.yaml；保留语义，不修改 minimal-rl 及其哈希。
  25 |    - 在 packages/code/src/presets/load.ts 的 PRESET_NAMES 登记两者，使 @agnes/code 的 presets 导出包含新 recipe。
  26 |    - 核对 packages/code/test/presets.test.ts 等枚举/文档检查，更新必要测试；不要删掉断言让测试通过。
  27 |    - 读取当前构建文档，重新构建 CLI。将变更提交在团队自己的 fork/分支，保存 commit 和补丁说明；不能给主办方上游推送。
  28 |    - 重新核验 SDK 接口后更新 HealthThread 的 agents/harness.lock.json。不能通过放宽 build-agh-sdk.mjs 的版本检查解决不匹配，也不能仅记录原始 HEAD 来掩盖已修改源码。
  29 |    - 在实际 AGH_HOME/profiles/local-dev/profile.yaml 的 profile 层合并 presets.default 和 presets.allowed；它只负责选择，不承载 preset 定义。不要覆盖账户配置或手改 configuration.json。
  30 |    - 开发实例 default 为 healththread-dev，产品实例 default 为 healththread-runtime。使用两个不同的绝对 AGH_HOME；核对实例连接目标，不能假定改 cwd 就切换 daemon。
  31 | 6. 对目标 preset 运行 AGH 的真实 schema 验证、继承解析和实际会话检查。JSON 合法不等于已装配，已装配不等于权限已隔离。列出可用工具及各槽路由，验证子任务不能再派生，第三个并行子任务被限制。优先用 fixture 验证，不为测试并发上限反复付费调用模型。
  32 | 7. worktree 从已提交 HEAD 建立，不会带上未提交 contracts/prompt；总控分派之前要提交任务所需基线。检查返回 details.isolation/worktreeSkipped，实际退回 shared 时不得两个写代码 Agent 并发。保留工作树，collect 不自动合并代码。
  33 | 8. 费用参数按实际 credits 设置；budget 不是 tokens 或人民币。schema 中 tree_budget_credits 和 per_request_cap 的单位也要核实。两份模板没有替用户决定 credits 数值，不能据此声称费用已被控制。步数 36/12、超时与输出长度是起始建议，应回写 D09；达到限制就保存进度、明确失败/中断，再续跑。
